89:23 added no separate branch-level correction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_32fe258769d09f881d3f","text":"89:16 retrospectively sharpened this ayah: the one who complains over measured provision refuses to become a channel of provision for the needy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B002","root_000726/B006","root_000726/B010","root_000934/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_595b6584d0d641619ce4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:18:4\",\"root_000934\",[\"B004\"]],[\"89:18:5\",\"root_000726\",[\"B002\",\"B006\",\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yoksula yiyecek ulaştırmak, onun ihtiyaç yüzünden hareketsiz kalmasını aşarak yerleşip hayatını sürdürebilmesini sağlayan maddi zemini kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000560/B002","root_000726/B002","root_000726/B010","root_000934/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_671af7c757a7f13f3f6c","text":"2. **Food as settlement-enabling provision.** Lexical evidence: `ط ع م B004` provision/livelihood/good condition; `س ك ن B002` dwelling and settlement; `س ك ن B010` provisions that enable staying; `ر ز ق B002` nourishing food in 89:16. Structural cue: provision misread in 89:16 is followed by refusal to mobilize food for the poor. Assigned role: food is what lets a vulnerable person remain settled rather than collapse into displacement. Abductive move: I infer that feeding repairs the social dwelling-space damaged by `فساد في البلاد`. Reading change: `طعام المسكين` becomes infrastructural provision, not a single meal-image only.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000334/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_691a2f0bb48d29c13414","text":"1. **Feeding as a missing network of mutual activation.** **Evidence/cue:** `ح ض ض B001` is urging and mutual exhortation, while `ط ع م B002` is feeding another and `ط ع م B004` extends food to provision, livelihood, and legal share. The plural form precedes consumption of inheritance and love of wealth. **Mechanism:** exhortation is the coordinating layer that turns individually held resources into collective provision; without it, the later acquisitive acts face no social counter-pressure. The packet supplies omission then accumulation; I infer the blocked coordination arrow. **Reading change:** the fault is not only failure to hand over a meal but failure to maintain the communal process that makes sustenance move.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000334/B001","root_000726/B006","root_000934/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_795fe1854259b74f1fa0","text":"1. **Failure of communal incitement, not only individual feeding.** Lexical evidence: `ح ض ض B001` urging/exhorting; `ط ع م B002` feeding and asking for food; `س ك ن B006` poverty, need, humble abasement. Structural cue: the plural verb condemns a community that does not mutually urge. Assigned role: urging is the missing social mechanism by which food reaches the needy. Abductive move: I infer that the fault is systemic, because the verb targets encouragement rather than merely the act of giving. Reading change: the ayah reads as a critique of public motivation and norms.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e2740386812d95e85624","text":"2. **Food as the means of restoring a stable place.** **Evidence/cue:** the surface *miskin* activates `س ك ن B006`, poverty and abasement, while `س ك ن B001/B002/B010` supplies cessation, dwelling, and provision that enables staying. 89:21-22 later replaces unstable social arrangement with leveled ground and straight rows. **Mechanism:** feeding gives the poor person enough material stability to dwell and act rather than remain immobilized by need. The packet supplies deprivation and spatial ordering; the restoration from food to stability is inferred. **Reading change:** *taʿam al-miskin* becomes support for social emplacement, not a momentary charitable object.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
